- **gmail-watcher**: Monitor Gmail inbox for new unread/important emails and create action files in the Obsidian vault. - **gmail-workspace**: Read, list, and search work email from Google Workspace Gmail using browser OAuth, the Gmail API, and a private local full-text cache. Use for checking a work inbox, finding messages by keyword/sender/Gmail query, reading mail, or downloading attachments. Strictly read-only; cannot send, draft, mark read, change labels, or delete mail. First-use setup guides users through creating their own OAuth Desktop client; no app password or domain-wide delegation. ([lukeramsden/gmail-workspace-agent-skill](https://github.com/lukeramsden/gmail-workspace-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/gmail-workspace))
